XRP to $1.3? Kalshi Traders Make Weak November Bets

📅 2026-11-15  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

XRP has continued to fall to levels not seen in the last three months as prolonged market volatility continues to intensify, causing the asset to post bigger daily price losses.

While momentum appears to be fading rapidly, the slow price movements have caused expectations for a November recovery to weaken, especially among prediction market traders.

Latest data from leading prediction market Kalshi shows that XRP traders have little confidence that the asset can still reclaim $1.30 before the end of the month.

While the asset saw higher expectations from Kalshi traders last month, it appears that its recent price dip below $1 has lowered their expectations as traders are assigning low probabilities to XRP reaching higher price targets this month.

Following their predictions, XRP currently has 7% odds of trading above $1.40, while the probability of reaching $1.50 stands at 8%.

More notably, bets on XRP surging to around $1.60 are even weaker, with only a 4% implied probability, suggesting that traders have increasingly lost confidence in XRP’s potential to achieve a major recovery before this month runs out.

The weak XRP predictions from Kalshi traders have come as XRP struggles to maintain momentum around its recent price levels, falling substantially below its local highs.

As of the time of writing, XRP is trading around $0.9892, representing a substantial decline of about 3.94% over the past 24 hours and an 11.3% drop over the last week.

It is important to note that the Kalshi data only reflects traders' positioning amid current market conditions and therefore does not guarantee XRP’s precise price movement in the future.

While XRP has continued to struggle below the major $1 level, it would need to stage a major recovery to reclaim the $1.30 level.

Calciomercato no stop - Indiscrezioni, trattative e retroscena del 14 novembre

📅 Nov 15, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

La Juventus guarda anche a Gustavo Varela per rinforzare il proprio reparto offensivo. L'attaccante portoghese del Monza, attuale capocannoniere della Serie A con 9 reti, è finito nel mirino dei bianconeri, alla ricerca di un centravanti da mettere a disposizione di Luciano Spalletti già nel mercato di gennaio. Come riferisce il Corriere dello Sport, Varela rappresenta una possibile alternativa qualora la dirigenza juventina non riuscisse a concretizzare un'operazione per un attaccante di maggiore esperienza. Il suo rendimento ha attirato l'attenzione di numerose società, rendendo però particolarmente complicata un'eventuale trattativa. Si tratta di Napoli in Serie A, ma anche club esteri come Barcellona, Borussia Dortmund e Lipsia.

Como attento ai talenti che si mettono in mostra in giro per il mondo. Uno di questi è Hamza Abdelkarim, punta classe 2008 di nazionalità egiziana, legato al Barcellona da un contratto valido fino al 30 giugno 2030. Il giocatore starebbe valutando di lasciare il club blaugrana dopo aver faticato a ritagliarsi uno spazio in prima squadra (appena 12 minuti tra tutte le competizioni) sotto la guida di Hansi Flick. Cesc Fabregas, al contrario, potrebbe garantirgli un impiego superiore con due soli attaccanti in rosa (Douvikas e Kean), mentre dal canto suo Abdelkarim non sarebbe così propenso a rimanere nella cantera del Barça a 18 anni.

Il PSV Eindhoven insiste per Kevin De Bruyne, ma il Napoli non ha intenzione, almeno per il momento, di privarsi del centrocampista belga. Il club olandese vorrebbe provare a convincere l'ex Manchester City a trasferirsi già a gennaio, anche se l'operazione si presenta complicata. Come riferisce il Corriere dello Sport, la posizione della società azzurra è chiara: De Bruyne resta un elemento importante per Massimiliano Allegri, che vorrebbe continuare ad averlo a disposizione nella seconda parte della stagione.

"Possono pagarti quanto vogliono, ma lo stress è tuo, del tuo corpo”. Parlava così Stanislav Lobotka prima dell'ultimo infortunio muscolare che lo ha fatto fermare per sei settimane. Possibile un suo rientro intorno a metà dicembre, ma nel frattempo il Napoli deve fare di necessità virtù, utilizzando Gilmour come schermo davanti alla difesa. Non è la stessa cosa, ovviamente. Lo slovacco percepiva 2,5 milioni di euro all'inizio della sua avventura a Napoli, mentre nel 2024 ha contrattato uno stipendio variabile verso l'alto fino a 4,5 milioni. Una cifra che per il club di De Laurentiis incomincia a essere troppo alta, al netto del Decreto Crescita: Lobotka difficilmente sarà una plusvalenza ed è possibile che vada via a parametro zero, nell'ottica di rifondare e cambiare la filosofia contrattuale. Sulla falsariga di quello che succederà ad Anguissa, insomma, la sensazione è che sia l'ultima stagione per Lobotka a Napoli.

Attenzione al futuro di Hakan Calhanoglu. Il centrocampista turco è a scadenza di contratto con l'Inter e come normale che sia la sua situazione inizia ad attrarre gli occhi indiscreti di chi lo prenderebbe volentieri con sé a parametro zero. In particolare negli ultimi giorni si è discusso dell'interesse della Juventus, oltre che di quello del Como. Al momento però c'è un ma importante dato che il calciatore, almeno fino a questo momento, dà priorità all'Inter. Il club avrebbe poi promesso al giocatore e ai suoi agenti di voler parlare di rinnovo attraverso una formula con più bonus e uno stipendio fisso un po' più basso. Bisogna però poi trovare gli accordi poiché Calhanoglu ha la testa all'Inter e i dirigenti gli hanno promesso di sedersi a un tavolo per trovare una soluzione. Si vedrà dunque come proseguirà la situazione.

Luis Henrique è tornato ad allenarsi in gruppo e sarà a disposizione di Cristian Chivu per la trasferta contro l'Atalanta. Il recupero dall'infortunio, tuttavia, non sembra destinato a cambiare le gerarchie sulla fascia nerazzurra. Secondo Tuttosport, l'ex Marsiglia rischia di restare ai margini anche nelle prossime settimane, complice la crescita dei concorrenti. Djed Spence si è ormai imposto sulla destra, mentre Andy Diouf rappresenta un'alternativa preziosa anche a gara in corso. Sull'altra corsia, Federico Dimarco sta ritrovando continuità e rendimento, con Carlos Augusto che ha sfruttato le opportunità ricevute, dimostrando di poter incidere sia da esterno sia da braccetto. Una concorrenza che ha progressivamente ridotto gli spazi per il brasiliano. Un impiego limitato che potrebbe spingere il giocatore a valutare nuove opportunità già nella finestra invernale. In estate la Roma aveva mostrato interesse, mentre in precedenza il nome dell'esterno era finito anche sul taccuino di Tiago Pinto per il Bournemouth. Il brasiliano, tuttavia, non aveva manifestato particolare entusiasmo per la destinazione giallorossa e aveva respinto gli approcci dalla Turchia, preferendo attendere eventuali proposte dalla Premier League. Lo scarso minutaggio a Milano potrebbe ora portarlo a rivedere le proprie priorità.

La Roma lavora per rinforzare la rosa in vista della sessione di mercato e cerca quel trequartista sinistro di piede destro che Gian Piero Gasperini chiede fin dal suo arrivo nella Capitale. Tra i diversi tavoli aperti dal ds Tony D'Amico, uno dei principali porta a Londra, sponda Chelsea, per Jamie Gittens. Il Chelsea potrebbe aprire a una cessione in prestito con diritto o obbligo di riscatto, sebbene a Stamford Bridge credano ancora nelle sue qualità mostrate in passato con il Borussia Dortmund. La Roma valuta attentamente le condizioni fisiche del giocatore, chiuso nella rosa inglese da profili come Neto, Estevao, Quenda e Rogers, ma resta fortemente interessata a riportarlo al centro del progetto tecnico di Gasperini per rinforzare la corsia offensiva sinistra nella seconda parte della stagione calcistica.

In casa Napoli, qualcosa potrebbe accadere per gli esterni visto che Lang è in uscita (anche se per il momento non sono arrivate offerte) ma qualche dubbio c’è anche su Neres, su cui c’è molto interesse in Brasile. E allora ecco che in questo caso ci sarebbe modo di inserire un giocatore con quel tipo di caratteristiche. Il profilo che piace di più è sicuramente Cacciamani già cercato anche dalla Roma in estate. Operazione davvero difficile anche perché il club granata non ha certo intenzione di cederlo a metà stagione. Un’altra soluzione è Gittens, che il Chelsea potrebbe cedere in prestito o magari anche a condizioni vantaggiose. Insomma il Napoli è al lavoro, a breve partiranno le grandi manovre.

Il primo posto in classifica non può che far sognare. Nessuno avrebbe mai immaginato che la Lazio dopo undici giornate fosse al comando della Serie A con 29 punti, frutto di ben 9 vittorie e due pareggi. Unica squadra ancora imbattuta. Una soddisfazione enorme, che adesso porta a nuove considerazioni anche sul mercato, sempre che a gennaio si verifichi la possibilità. I biancocelesti devono infatti ancora capire come potranno muoversi ma intanto c’è una certezza: tutti i big resteranno. Per cui chi volesse avvicinarsi ai vari Taylor, Nuno Tavares, Isaksen e tutti gli altri può tranquillamente mettersi l’animo in pace. Insomma nessuna cessione e magari qualche pensiero per un eventuale inserimento. Al momento l’idea Icardi rimane tale, nel senso che non ci sono mai stati veramente passi concreti ma solo sondaggi senza mai entrare nello specifico. Questo però non significa che il presidente Lotito non abbia in mente di rafforzare la squadra magari con un altro esterno offensivo. Un giocatore che piace è sicuramente Dennis Man del PSV Ehindoven, con un passato al Parma a cui non dispiacerebbe la possibilità di tornare a giocare in Serie A.

Due assist e un gol per Donyell Malen, addirittura tripletta per Tijjani Reijnders. L'Olanda di Xavi si è divertita a ridicolizzare la Grecia 7-0 ieri nel confronto valido il quinto turno di UEFA Nations League, salendo a quota 11 punti con vista sulla qualificazione ai quarti di finale - anche se tutto dipenderà dall'incrocio finale con la Germania di Klopp. Ma davvero rimarrà all'Al Qadsiah? I numeri in Saudi Pro League sono da top player, vice capocannoniere con ben 8 gol alla sua prima esperienza lontano dall'Europa e in Medio Oriente. Anche davanti all'ex Atalanta e oggi compagno di squadra Mateo Retegui.

Il nome di Georges Mikautadze è risuonato con forza nella fase finale del calciomercato in vista di un possibile arrivo al Barcellona, quando la dirigenza catalana continuava a non riuscire a ingaggiare Julian Alvarez. Il georgiano di proprietà del Villarreal - con tanto di contratto duraturo fino al 2031 - non è finito dove avrebbe voluto, ma ciò non ha intaccato il grande affetto che continua a provare per i colori blaugrana. Lo ha affermato l’attaccante in un’intervista a DAZN. Mikautadze, che a poco a poco si sta facendo un nome sempre più importante al Villarreal, è stato interrogato sull’interesse mostrato dal Barça durante l’estate e il calciatore non ha esitato a mostrare il suo grande affetto per la squadra guidata da Hansi Flick. "È lusinghiero. Il Barça è il club del mio cuore, lo guardo giocare da quando ero piccolo. Continuo a seguire le sue partite anche adesso", ha commentato l’attaccante 26enne, autore di 5 gol in questa stagione, di cui tre in Liga e altri due in Champions League.

Nel mondo esiste un altro Ronal­di­nho? A rispondere alla domanda ci penso direttamente il fuoriclasse brasiliano, che sta regalando emozioni in queste ora ai tifosi del Ravenna e più in generale a tutti gli appassionati di calcio che potranno rivedere in campo l'ex campione di Barcellona e Milan domani contro il Forlì: "Non mi piace fare para­goni - ha sottolineato lo stesso Ronaldinho al Corriere della Sera -. Ci sono tanti gio­ca­tori che mi piac­ciono, i bra­si­liani per esem­pio". E a proposito di brasiliani, inevitabile parlare di Endrick, attaccante del Real Madrid che piace per gennaio a Milan e Roma: "Se è pronto per la Serie A? Può gio­care in ogni cam­pio­nato", sottolinea Ronaldinho che poi a La Gazzetta dello Sport aggiunge: "È un ragazzo che può giocare in tutte le parti del mondo: ha fatto tanti campionati e bene, in Francia, Brasile e Spagna, è molto giovane e può giocare facendo bene sì...".

Il Benfica blinda Gianluca Prestianni. Manuel Rui Costa ha confermato infatti che il club portoghese è ormai vicino all'accordo per il rinnovo del giovane attaccante argentino, già autore di 9 gol e 6 assist in 16 presenze ufficiali con gli Encarnados quest'anno. Il presidente delle Aquile ha parlato del futuro del classe 2006 a margine dell'inaugurazione della 256ª Casa do Benfica a Cantanhede, dove ha risposto alle domande dei giornalisti sui principali temi di attualità della società. "È praticamente tutto pronto per il rinnovo e andrà in porto senza alcun problema. Firmerà a breve, è tutto già sistemato", ha dichiarato Rui Costa, confermando che le trattative sono ormai alle battute finali.

Calvin and Hobbes’ 5 Greatest Spaceman Spiff Comic Strips

📅 Nov 15, 2026  |  Category: Other  |  Read Source →

Send this article to your favorite space crustacean

Send this article to your favorite space crustacean

Calvin and Hobbes wasn't Bill Watterson's first pitch for a comic strip. C&H's protagonists began as side characters in Watterson's comic The Doghouse before his original syndicate suggested that they ought to be the focus. "Marvin" became "Calvin," the rest of The Doghouse's cast was dropped, and while United Feature Syndicate still didn't take the resulting comic, Universal Press Syndicate did.

Before then, Watterson had drawn cartoons about a spacefaring character as early as college. Watterson went on to make that character the focus of one of his rejected comics: a mustachioed, cigar-smoking parody of adventurers like Flash Gordon. Shortly after Calvin and Hobbes began, Watterson reworked "Mort" into Calvin's fantasy alter ego Spaceman Spiff.

Over the course of Calvin and Hobbes' run, Spiff was one of Calvin's most frequent escapist characters. Whenever Calvin was caught in a boring or unpleasant situation, he frequently retreated into his Spiff persona to pass time or even make a hasty retreat. In practice, that gave Watterson a handy excuse to take Calvin and Hobbes' art well outside its usual mundane setting, with tons of gross aliens, impossible landscapes, and great phrases like "eat hot electric death" that don't usually come up in everyday conversation.

Much like the rest of Calvin and Hobbes, Spaceman Spiff really hit his stride in the comic's last three years, when Watterson began doing his trademark "unbreakable" Sunday strips. Some of the most colorful and evocative C&H comics are Spaceman Spiff adventures from the mid-'90s, but Spiff made a few solid appearances before that point. Here are some of our picks for the best Spaceman Spiff comics in Calvin and Hobbes.

When Calvin gets sick during his summer vacation, his first reaction is to get annoyed. Sick days are for the school year. I think we all can relate.

Unfortunately for all involved, Calvin's mom takes him to the doctor. That includes a checkup, an injection, and a retreat into Calvin's Spiff mode, as he imagines himself as a prisoner of aliens rather than a little kid trapped in an examination room.

Calvin's teacher Mrs. Wormwood catches a lot of strays in Spaceman Spiff cartoons. Sometimes I want to see a version of C&H from her perspective. Most of her class are apparently boring normal kids who do the reading and don't get in unusual amounts of trouble. Then there's Calvin, who's as much of a chaos agent as a six-year-old can be. Mrs. Wormwood and Mr. Wilson from Dennis the Menace would have a lot to talk about.

In this early installation, Calvin gets called on in class and seamlessly incorporates Mrs. Wormwood's history question into his daydream. It's not being called on in class; it's being interrogated by the Hideous Zorgs of Planet X-13. (Look at those bug-men. Those are aliens that deserve the word "Hideous" being part of their proper noun.)

The most extreme (in the '90s sense) Spaceman Spiff adventure was a week-long story where Calvin got deeply enough into character that he staged a breakout from elementary school. Called to the board to answer a math problem, Calvin instead decides to shoot his way out (with a rubber band), then run for the door.

Through what one assumes is the relative indifference of school staff, Calvin manages to make it all the way home before his mom catches him. Unfortunately, Spiff can't get Calvin out of the ensuing consequences.

Right from the start, Bill Watterson has said that one of his primary influences on Calvin & Hobbes is George Herriman's Golden Age newspaper comic Krazy Kat, which ran from 1913 to 1944. That was already some inside baseball in the 1980s when Calvin and Hobbes was new, as Krazy Kat’s modern following is almost exclusively made up of historians, fanatics, and other cartoonists. It’s not dead media yet, though, as historians and preservationists have made a point of keeping collections of Krazy Kat in print. Curious readers can check out Fantagraphics' George Herriman Library hardcover collections, which are still in print at time of writing.

If you check out Krazy Kat, its influence on Calvin and Hobbes is obvious immediately, particularly in its colored Sunday comics. Herriman's characters inhabited a cartoonish version of the Arizona desert, and many of Watterson's Spaceman Spiff strips feature him visiting alien planets with similar arid backgrounds. This largely-wordless strip from 1993 is a perfect example, with so much of it devoted to Calvin's imagined spaceship journey from an alien valley to home.

One of the best Spaceman Spiff strips comes in the last year of Calvin and Hobbes. Without dialogue, we see the entire comic from Calvin's perspective, as he's entertaining himself during yet another boring class by imagining himself behind the stick of Spiff's flying saucer.

Suddenly, Mrs. Wormwood interrupts his daydream and breaks his train of thought. As soon as she isn't paying attention anymore, Calvin switches to a new scenario: this time, he's a dinosaur stalking prey.

We want to hear from you. Share your perspective in the comments below, and please keep the conversation respectful.

Adani parla di Ramos: "Lo vedo pensiero di Amorim. È la punta di diamante di tutte le verticalizzazioni"

📅 Nov 15, 2026  |  Category: Other  |  Read Source →

Nel corso del consueto appuntamento con 'Viva El Futbol', programma che conduce sulla piattaforma streaming in compagnia di Antonio Cassano e Nicola Ventola, l'ex calciatore Daniele Adani ha parlato di Milan soffermandosi in modo particolare su Gonçalo Ramos, difendendolo:

"Fa crescere la squadra ma è troppo indietro con il numero di gol. E alle volte il numero di gol, oltre a dare fiducia a lui, ti risolve alcune situazioni per lavorare un po' meglio, anche per lui. Io lo so che è molto facile, anche giusto essere scettici e criticare. Prego di convicervi che sa giocare al calcio ed è secondo me giustamente ambizioso senza essere egoista, valora aggiunto in una squadra che sta crescendo e che si sta affermando. Lo vedo nel pensiero di Ruben Amorim, anche nel pensiero di gioco, dove tu sei un vertice di quattro linee dietro di te, ed è come se fosse la punta di diamante di tutte le verticalizzazioni. Ti dico che lo vedo felice anche se non realizza"

Netflix Officially Brings Back a Hit 3-Part Rom-Com Series

📅 Nov 15, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

Netflix has built a strong foundation of romance titles over the years. 2026 alone saw the return of some of their heaviest hitters, with Bridgerton returning for Season 4 towards the beginning of the year and Heartstopper officially wrapping up with the release of its feature-length finale, Heartstopper Forever. The teen drama My Life With the Walter Boys also welcomed viewers back to Silver Falls with Season 3, with plenty of swoon-worthy moments to boot. When it comes to rom-com series specifically, though, there's only one that everybody wants more of.

Netflix has officially renewed its red-hot story of star-crossed lovers for another run as of November 6. It's part of a surprising run of renewals for the streamer this month, alongside Adults, Youth, Outlast, and, in a history-maker for the platform, Virgin River, which will become its longest-running scripted drama by a number of seasons at nine. Nobody Wants This continuing is no shocker, given the viewership and Season 3 rating as its second-best on Rotten Tomatoes with a Certified Fresh score of 83% from critics. Erin Foster, Jenni Konner, and Bruce Eric Kaplan will be back as co-showrunners, writing, "We are so happy and thrilled to do another season of our favorite show on television, which coincidentally is the one we run."

Four love stories, four completely different ideas of what it means to be somebody's person. This is the one that matches how you actually fall, and what it would ask of you.

You do not fall for strangers. You fall for the person you have already been talking to for years, which is the slowest and by some distance the most durable way anybody has found to do this.

You want a partner, not an audience. Somebody who wants their own life as badly as you want yours, and who would never ask you to be smaller to make the relationship easier.

The intensity was never the problem. The secrecy is. You are drawn to a love that lives entirely in private, and to the particular ache of wanting somebody you are not supposed to want.

You do not do this cautiously. When it happens for you it is immediate and total and it rearranges everything, and you would not accept a smaller version of it.

Season 3 ended with wedding bells ringing for Noah and Joanne on the beach in a surprise ceremony in front of no one but God and a random man walking his dog nearby. What was initially meant as a trip to finalize Joanne's conversion to Judaism turns into a sacred moment for the couple that binds them together forever. However, they still have an entire squabbling family to break the news to, all of whom are sure to be very normal about it.

There's also the reason for their bickering to deal with — Sasha is caught between his estranged wife Esther and Morgan, with both finding out about his hook-ups with the other. His story is expected to get a big spotlight in Season 4, with all of Stephanie Faracy, Michael Hitchcock, Paul Ben-Victor, Tovah Feldshuh, Andrew Rannells, Sarah Silverman, Shiloh Bearman, and Sherry Cola likely returning alongside the aforementioned stars.

History Says This Is the Best Investing Move You Can Make if a Bear Market Is Coming

📅 Nov 15, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Should investors wait for stocks to fall before buying?

Right now, the U.S. stock market feels a little shaky. Inflation is still well above target. The Federal Reserve is on track to raise interest rates again next month. And the Iran war shows no signs of ending.

The S&P 500 (^GSPC -0.40%) is down a modest 3% in the last month, while the Nasdaq-100 is off nearly 10%.

All of this is to say that many investors are considering the possibility of a bear market right now. While earnings expectations for U.S. companies are still looking strong, there are enough concerns about the future of the artificial intelligence (AI) building boom, the biggest driver of recent economic growth, that investors are looking at ways to protect themselves.

And it's a good thing, too. The best time to think about your investment process is before something happens.

Market declines tend to trigger some of the worst investor behaviors. If stocks are declining, there's an inclination to stay away because 10% drawdowns often become 20% to 30%. It happened in 2000-2002, 2007-2009, and 2022.

But those declines also have a recent history of rebounding quickly, often before investors even noticed. These occurred in late 2018 and early 2025 during President Donald Trump's Liberation Day tariffs, and in the more extreme case of the COVID pandemic.

In other words, nobody knows whether a correction is the end or the beginning of a deeper drawdown. And even if you did know which one it was going to be, market timing requires you to be right twice: once when you decide to get out of the market and once when you later get back in. Plus, that requires a discipline many investors lack.

You can still take advantage of lower share prices during bear markets by simply maintaining your monthly investing schedule. If you have money going into a brokerage account every month or every paycheck into something like a 401(k), keep doing what you're doing.

This process allows you to buy the dips and lower your average cost without worrying about whether it's the right time to trade. Plus, bear markets often last far less time than the average bull market.

If you try to time the market, there's a good chance that the rebound will have already started once you decide it's OK to return to stocks. By automating, you may not get the absolute lowest prices on your purchases, but you'll certainly get at least some at discounted prices.

As always, investing is one of the few things where doing less can yield better results.

When our analyst team has a stock tip, it can pay to listen. After all, Stock Advisor's total average return is 823%* — a market-crushing outperformance compared to 209% for the S&P 500.

They just revealed what they believe are the 10 best stocks for investors to buy right now, available when you join Stock Advisor.

GTA 6's 212GB PS5 File Size Prompts Madden NFL 27 to Beg You Not to Delete It

📅 Nov 15, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Any issues with GTA 6’s 212GB install size have largely been mitigated by Rockstar’s decision to let you preload the game a week in advance.

The sheer scale of the open world title is causing potential headaches for other game publishers, however.

A new PS5 console comes with a 1TB SSD, and while you can expand that, I’d imagine the vast majority of users don’t.

That means alongside the operating system, approximately a quarter of your system’s storage will be taken up by GTA 6 alone.

This is a problem for other game publishers, who are probably going to see their titles deleted to make room.

And in a new message added to Madden NFL 27, publisher EA Sports has urged its users not to delete the game:

In a distinctly GTA 6-style message, the game says: “Don’t delete! The crew is assembling for a high-stakes run. Await the signal from our insider on 11/17. Do not leak this transmission!”

So it seems the game is teasing some kind of content update, presumably focused on Ultimate Team, which is scheduled to deploy on 17th November – two days before GTA 6.

EA wrote a similar message on social media earlier this week, saying: “Don't let 210GB get between you and Your League.”

I’m sure all this is intended to be taken tongue in cheek, but retaining players once Rockstar’s game comes out is probably a very real problem for devs running live services.

I’m sure those who enjoy playing American football will be back eventually, regardless of whether the client gets temporarily deleted or not.

She-Hulk's Tatiana Maslany Starred In A Must-Watch 2026 Apple TV Series

📅 Nov 15, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Tatiana Maslany's career exploded in the mid-2010s with her virtuoso multi-character work on BBC America's brainy sci-fi thriller series "Orphan Black." It was a very good show, but the deeper Maslany got into her roles, the more you wondered what she could do in a film or series where her performance prowess could be concentrated on one specific part. Indeed, she proved to be unsettlingly fierce as the vengeful Sister Alice on HBO's dark reinvention of "Perry Mason" and brought all the charm she could muster to Marvel Studios' irreverent "She-Hulk: Attorney at Law." Her talent was undeniable, but the projects weren't proper showcases of her astonishing ability.

This changed in 2026 with the premiere of Apple TV's comedy-thriller series "Maximum Pleasure Guaranteed." The show stars Maslany as Paula Saunders, a fact checker (the noblest and most under-appreciated of journalistic professions) who's fighting for custody of her child while looking into the kidnapping and apparent death of a webcam model named Trevor (Brandon Flynn). No less an authority than Stephen King has effused that "Maximum Pleasure Guaranteed" is "like Hitchcock came back to do it one more time." Its first season is streaming in its entirety on Apple TV, and it's officially coming back for more. So, now is the time to get caught up before you're 20 episodes deep down a binge hole.

The tone-setting pilot of "Maximum Pleasure Guaranteed" was directed by David Gordon Green, who was once a Terrence Malick-ian stylist (see: early triumphs like "George Washington" and "All the Real Girls") before he segued to mainstream movies via broad stoner comedies like "Pineapple Express" and "Your Highness." But while "Maximum Pleasure Guaranteed" possesses a bit of visual sophistication, it's the writing by series creator David J. Rosen and his staff that puts it over the top.

The casting is also key. Jake Johnson is deliciously antagonistic as Paula's ex-husband Karl, who's seeking sole custody of their child while she nurses her anguish with wine and digs into the fate of her newfound internet companion. Rosen and the show's writers pile the complications on with inventive abandon, and Tatiana Maslany surprises every step of the way as a protagonist we simply can't figure out.

"Maximum Pleasure Guaranteed," as mentioned, has been renewed for a second season, so Maslany will get another 10 episodes to explore possibly the best character she's ever been given to play. In the meantime, you should get out to see Noah Segan's "The Only Living Pickpocket in New York," where she is dynamite as John Turturro's daughter. The Maslany-ssiance is upon us.

There's a hidden way you can check which services are running on your Android phone

📅 Nov 15, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Just like a desktop PC, your Android phone has a built-in task manager. Here is how to find it, monitor your memory and safely limit background apps.

If you use a desktop PC, you may be familiar with the task manager, which lets you view all the processes running in the background of your computer and taking up resources. What you may not know is that Android phones (even budget models) have the same feature, but it's kept hidden away from users, requiring a little digging to find it. If you want to keep an eye on what's using your phone's memory, it can be worth uncovering this hidden display, but you should proceed with caution before you start closing processes willy-nilly.

To check what processes are running, you first need to activate developer mode to make your phone faster. Under the Settings menu, scroll down to the bottom, tap About Phone and look for the Build number. If it's not there, you may have to enter an additional Software information menu, depending on your phone's model and Android version. Once you find it, keep tapping the Build number option until you get a message that developer mode has turned on or a dialog box asking you to confirm. Then, back out to the main Settings menu. You'll find Developer options on this main screen, or under a System submenu. Tap the option, then open Running Services. You can also select the three dots in the top right of the screen to select Show cached processes and see what's been recently suspended but still held in memory.

This is Android's version of the task manager, showing you all the running services currently providing frameworks for the apps on your phone, along with how much memory they're using. But don't start closing processes to free up that precious memory. While the task manager on PC often shows plenty of bloat that you can safely close, that's not usually the case for Android phones. The processes you see here are more likely managing features like Bluetooth, your keyboard and location settings. Shutting these down can seriously hamper your phone.

Both the Running Services and Memory menus are more like diagnostic tools. If you're having problems with your phone, it can be helpful to check here and see if anything seems wildly out of order, like a single process hogging the majority of your phone's memory. You may want to stop the related process until you can fix the underlying issue, but be prepared to restart your phone if anything goes wrong.

A better way of controlling background processes is by limiting their use. In the Developer options menu, under the Apps heading, you'll find Background process limit. This lets you limit the number of apps allowed to run in the background at one time. This default setting triggers an automatic limit based on how much RAM your phone has, which is a crucial factor in making older Android phones last longer. However, you can also change the limit to between one and four to free up memory.

Keep in mind that this will make multitasking harder and will cause a bigger performance hit at the moment you re-open apps, since your phone will have to fully restart them now.

You can also limit how individual apps behave in the background. In the Settings menu, head to Apps, select a specific app and tap Battery. The default Optimized option automatically adjusts the app's background permissions based on how you use it, but switching to Restricted limits its activity when suspended.

Cowboys vs 49ers: 2 players to buy, 2 to sell against San Francisco

📅 Nov 15, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

The Dallas Cowboys are coming off a narrow win over the Indianapolis Colts, and now the 7-1 San Francisco 49ers are coming to town, bringing one of the league's most complete rosters and a defense that has consistently given Dallas problems in their recent matchups.

There are some opportunities for the Cowboys, particularly with CeeDee Lamb playing at a high level, but there are also some difficult matchups that could expose weaknesses on Dallas' roster. Here are two players to buy and two to sell ahead of Sunday's game.

I mean, who else would this list start with? Lamb has been the best player for the Cowboys all season, and there's no reason to think that Dallas will stop feeding him the ball. Lamb is coming off a monster performance against Indianapolis, hauling in 10 catches for 195 yards and a touchdown on 16 targets. It was his second-highest receiving total in a game during his career.

Through nine games, Lamb has 70 receptions for 904 yards and seven touchdowns. He's already surpassed the 900-yard mark, and his 108 targets lead the Cowboys by a wide margin. The matchup against San Francisco is difficult, but Lamb is the kind of receiver who can change a game regardless of the opponent. With George Pickens possibly missing another week and Dallas dealing with inconsistencies elsewhere the offense, Dak Prescott should continue looking Lamb's way early and often.

The 49ers will undoubtedly have a plan for him, but Lamb's recent production makes him difficult to leave out of the buyers market. He's been the Cowboys' most reliable playmaker overall, and Dallas needs to find ways to get him involved.

At some point, the Cowboys have to figure out how to beat San Francisco, and Dak Prescott has to be a big part of that solution. Unfortunately, his history against the 49ers doesn't inspire much confidence. Prescott is 2-4 against San Francisco in his career, including an 0-2 record in the playoffs. In those two postseason games, he has thrown for 460 yards, two touchdowns and three interceptions. His most recent regular-season meetings haven't been much better. He threw three interceptions in the 2023 blowout loss and two more in the 2024 matchup.

Across his four regular-season games against San Francisco, Prescott has completed 65.5% of his passes for 875 yards, eight touchdowns and five interceptions. That's not necessarily disastrous production, but the turnovers have been costly, particularly against a team that knows how to capitalize on mistakes.

The 49ers have won four straight meetings against Dallas, and Prescott will have to overcome both that team history and his own struggles against this opponent. San Francisco's defense is disciplined, physical and capable of making quarterbacks pay for forcing throws.

With Fred Warner leading the unit, Prescott will have to be sharp with his reads and decision-making, especially against the blitz, where he has looked terrible this year. Dallas needs a big performance from its quarterback to pull off the upset. Given the history, though, this is a matchup where I'm selling Prescott until he proves otherwise.

With Jake Ferguson ruled out due to a knee injury, Brevyn Spann-Ford has an opportunity to take on an even bigger role in the Cowboys' offense. It might not be the most obvious matchup to exploit, but he could be a sneaky contributor against San Francisco.

Spann-Ford has caught 15 passes for 130 yards this season, and his role has been growing. Against Indianapolis, he played 55 offensive snaps compared to Ferguson's 27, catching two of three targets for 20 yards. That workload suggests the Cowboys are already comfortable giving him significant playing time.

The 49ers have plenty of defensive talent to account for, and their attention will naturally gravitate toward Lamb and the Cowboys' other primary playmakers. That could leave opportunities for Spann-Ford underneath or in the middle of the field, particularly if Dallas uses him to help with protection before releasing him into a route.

This isn't a prediction that Spann-Ford is suddenly going to become the focal point of the passing game. Rather, it's a bet that his increased playing time and the attention paid to Dallas' other weapons could create a few favorable opportunities. With Ferguson unavailable, the Cowboys need someone else to contribute from the tight end position. Spann-Ford is in a good position to do that.

The Cowboys have indicated that Jaishawn Barham could see an increased workload as injuries continue to pile up on the defensive side of the ball. That's understandable from a personnel standpoint, but it also creates a potential problem against an offense as versatile as San Francisco's.

Barham had some encouraging moments early in the season, but his limitations have become more apparent as the competition has increased. His playing time has also been limited recently, which makes the prospect of a larger role against the 49ers particularly concerning.

San Francisco is exactly the type of opponent that can put a young linebacker in difficult situations. Kyle Shanahan's offense uses motion, misdirection and mismatches to create space, and open up running lanes.

If Dallas gives him more responsibility, San Francisco will have plenty of opportunities to test him. The Cowboys need all the help they can get defensively, but this is a tough week to ask an inconsistent young player to take on a bigger role.

Could everything be broken? Grounded jets, Microsoft outage, AI chaos make some wonder

📅 Nov 15, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

"There's a lot of weird stuff going on," Chuck Amasio, a 68-year-old from Rahway, New Jersey, said. "It makes one think."

Maybe the trouble started with the AI billionaire and the Silicon Valley chaos that followed talk of his infidelity, including with an alleged Chinese spy. Or maybe it was the Microsoft outage that, depending on how you looked at it, either cast millions of frustrated workers adrift or spared them from a daily, unrelenting electronic avalanche.

But by Friday afternoon, when U.S. aviation authorities ordered the grounding of planes across the country, it felt to many that something was up. Something was broken.

Just ask Chuck Amasio, a 68-year-old from Rahway, New Jersey, trying to fly out of Newark Liberty International Airport when his canceled flight led to a few beers with his brother in an airport bar and eventually a night in a hotel. Saturday morning, he was back at the airport to try again.

“There’s a lot of weird stuff going on,” Amasio said. “It makes one think.”

His first thought was dark: “We talk about how wars aren’t done with bombs anymore. They shut down airspace or a power grid. They can cripple an economy.”

Throw in a few more examples just from Friday â a cruise ship that overturned in New Orleans when a tanker slammed into it, suspicious drones over a U.S. air base in Britain, a bomb found near an ethanol plant in Ohio â and perhaps things seem even weirder, opening the door for a writer who has been picking at America’s fears for generations to offer his own explanation.

“The aliens have landed,” Stephen King wrote in a Friday afternoon tweet. “That’s the REAL reason for the ground stop. They don’t want to start a worldwide panic.”

The official explanations for the thousands of grounded jetliners and hours of email and other computer troubles were far more prosaic.

A technology upgrade on the nation’s air traffic control system had overloaded antiquated equipment and caused “intermittent” communications and radar gaps, leading to the ground stop that lasted more than four hours, administration officials said.

By Saturday morning, air travel was already smoother and Bryan Bedford, administrator of the Federal Aviation Administration, said airline schedules should be back to normal by late Sunday.

Microsoft, for its part, said Saturday that its services had been fully restored, saying the problem “was introduced by an issue during an earlier network capacity upgrade that surfaced” following a recent network change.

The outage is believed to have affected many millions of people around the world.

Maxine Gustafson, a 34-year-old physician assistant from Minneapolis, was in line to pick up a hastily booked rental car at Chicago’s O’Hare airport on Friday afternoon so she and colleagues could get home from a work training.

Gustafson said the week’s mishaps made her feel afraid and helpless.

“It makes you think, like, is this a cyberattack?” she said. “We don’t have control over a lot of this stuff.”

Both the FAA and Microsoft said there were no signs that malicious activity had been behind the troubles.

Tori Perkins, 34, was hoping for a Saturday flight at Terminal 2 at San Diego International Airport, where 11 of the 62 flights already were listed as canceled that morning and the same number were delayed. Crowds were normal.

“It’s one of those things where I don’t necessarily believe what the government says,” said Perkins, who seemed to take the setback in stride. “I don’t know what’s true because there are so many people saying different things.”

Perkins, who lives in Hawaii, was in San Diego on a layover on her way to see family in Florida. She spent Friday night at the airport.

She was not surprised that Secretary of Transportation Sean Duffy had made a plea Friday for tens of billions in more funding to upgrade the country’s notoriously outdated air traffic system.

“It sounds like more of the same,” she said.

The FAA has already replaced thousands of copper wire connections with fiber optic lines and upgraded hundreds of radio systems as part of the overhaul that began last year after Congress approved $12.5 billion in funding. The White House has been pushing for $30 billion more to complete the work.

Sometimes chaos brings out the best in people.

Corbyn Wascher was stuck on the tarmac at Charlotte Douglas International Airport for four hours while heading back to Moline, Illinois, from Miami, where he had been at an education conference.

He said everyone was cordial on the plane, talking to one another and stretching their legs. He was impressed with the American Airlines staff, who handed out water, pretzels and biscotti snacks, though they were clearly under stress.

“The stewardesses were amazing,” said the 37-year-old high school teacher.

Back at the Newark airport, Caroline Ruiz had caught a few hours of sleep by a heating vent. She wasn’t sure what time it was as she waited for someone to bring her a meal she was promised after the cancellation of the first leg of her 24-hour trip to Australia. She hoped to get started later Saturday.

It was two days in a row, she noted, where she had been betrayed by technology.

Though maybe one of those days didn’t count as a betrayal.

“I kind of got a free day yesterday” because the Microsoft systems were down.By TIM SULLIVAN Associated Press

Cape Verde election could deliver first woman president since independence in 1975

📅 Nov 15, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

Polls closed Sunday evening in the vote in which former Justice Minister Joana Rosa is challenging President JosÃ© Maria Neves, who is seeking a second term.

PRAIA, Cape Verde (AP) â Cape Verde residents voted Sunday in a presidential election that could make Joana Rosa the country’s first woman head of state since independence from Portugal in 1975.

Polls closed Sunday evening in the vote in which the former justice minister is challenging President JosÃ© Maria Neves, who is seeking a second term. Six candidates are competing. If none wins more than half the votes, a runoff will be held Nov. 29.

Rosa has the backing of the opposition Movement for Democracy, which governed for a decade before losing May’s parliamentary election. Women have held senior Cabinet positions, but the Atlantic island nation off West Africa has never had a woman president or prime minister.

The archipelagic country in the Atlantic Ocean, off the coast of West Africa, is battling with challenges such as a high unemployment rate and cost of living.

“I see many promises that are not fulfilled. I am tired, but I am still voting,” Aderito Cardoso, a 44-year-old resident of Villa Nova in the capital city of Praia, said after casting her vote.

Under Cape Verde’s parliamentary system, the prime minister directs policy and holds most executive power. The directly elected president’s role is largely ceremonial, although the office carries the power to veto legislation.

The country of about half a million people has a record of competitive elections and peaceful transfers of power that stands out in the region. Nearly 77,000 citizens living abroad are registered to vote, accounting for almost a fifth of the electorate.

Roselma Ãvora, a professor of political science and gender studies at the University of Cabo Verde, said limited economic opportunities were pushing young people to leave. Many voters also feel disconnected from political leaders and want a president who understands their daily struggles, she said.

Neves, who won the presidency in 2021 with about 52% of the vote, has promised to build cross-party support to improve housing, healthcare and education, create jobs and raise wages.

His incumbency has given him greater visibility, including among overseas voters, Ãvora said. Rosa’s candidacy, meanwhile, challenges traditional expectations of women in a society that remains “very patriarchal.”

Rosa has focused her campaign on the balance of power, arguing that the presidency should serve as a counterweight to the African Party for the Independence of Cape Verde, which backs Neves and controls the government and most municipalities.

The party, known as PAICV, won a parliamentary majority in May, ending a decade of rule by the Movement for Democracy. A Rosa victory would again place the presidency and government in opposing political camps.

For Cape Verde-based writer RosÃ¡rio Luz, party loyalties matter more than Rosa’s gender.

“We have a lot of women in politics, but we don’t have the policies for women that we should have,” she said.

Women shoulder much of the financial responsibility for their households, but government policies have failed to address that burden, Luz said.

Megaspeed says Alice Huang left in 2025 as company conducts internal probe over Nvidia fallout

📅 Nov 15, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

A customer and cloud partner of Nvidia, Megaspeed has faced scrutiny in recent months, including from U.S. authorities, over allegations that it circumvented U.S. export restrictions to route large quantities of advanced chips to Chinese companies.

Singapore-based cloud computing company Megaspeed International said Sunday that Chinese businesswoman Alice Huang, whose relationship with former Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang sparked U.S. national security concerns, left the company last year.Megaspeed, which is under scrutiny over alleged transfers of advanced Nvidia chips to Chinese companies, also announced an internal investigation led by outside lawyers.

The company told The Associated Press that it runs a “legitimate and lawful business” and was aware of media reports containing claims about its business, supply chain and global operations “that are not supported by verifiable evidence.” It did not address specific allegations.

“The company has retained external legal counsel to conduct a thorough internal investigation,” it said in an emailed statement.

Alice Huang “left Megaspeed effective Aug. 30, 2025, and currently has no role with the company,” the statement said.

“Megaspeed does not comment on Ms. Huang’s personal and private matters.”

A customer and cloud partner of Nvidia, Megaspeed has faced scrutiny in recent months, including from U.S. authorities, over allegations that it circumvented U.S. export restrictions to route large quantities of advanced chips to Chinese companies. Last December, it said there was “no evidence” of illicit chip transfers.

Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent said Sunday that “it doesn’t matter to me” whether Alice Huang is a spy. He said his concern is “whether Nvidia had a culture of compliance around the U.S. export restrictions” or not.

“Jensen’s foibles are his foibles. Obviously, it’s a problem if in fact he was having relationships with subordinates,” Bessent said in an appearance on Fox News Channel’s “Sunday Morning Futures.” “I want to keep coming back to: ‘What was the culture at Nvidia, and were they pressing these seemingly illicit sales to goose their revenues and their earnings?’”

Bessent said the Nvidia board of directors should look outside the company for a new CEO because “they need a dramatic shake-up.”

Singapore’s government said Thursday that it initiated an investigation into Megaspeed in February 2025, including possible “false declarations in relation to the purchase of servers.”

Authorities also investigated Alice Huang, whom the government identified as Megaspeed’s then-CEO, chairperson and one of its key controllers.

Singapore authorities questioned her and withheld her passport last year. They later concluded there was insufficient evidence that she had committed a crime, despite suspecting she had provided “inaccurate” information to downplay her role at the company.

An earlier New York Times report suggested U.S. officials believed Alice Huang was a Chinese spy recruited to potentially influence Nvidia to make its advanced chips more available to China.

As scrutiny intensified and reports of her romantic relationship with Jensen Huang gained attention, Nvidia announced Thursday that he was stepping down as CEO.

U.S. Attorney General Todd Blanche said Thursday, however, that the Justice Department had not concluded whether Alice Huang was a Chinese spy. Asked whether she was a spy, President Donald Trump said: “I don’t know if that’s been determined yet.”

Beijing rejected the allegations as unsubstantiated.

The Associated Press has been unable to reach Alice Huang for comment.

Megaspeed began as the overseas arm of a Chinese video game developer and was incorporated in Singapore in 2015 as 7Road International. It adopted its current name in March 2023, when all its shares were transferred to Huang Le Limited, a British Virgin Islands company. Alice Huang is also known by her Chinese name, Huang Le.

By November 2023, she was no longer listed as a Megaspeed director, and all her shares had been transferred to an entity owned by Singaporean businessman Tan Yong Pong, also known as James Tan.

Despite those public records, short seller Culper Research cited a Megaspeed employee in a report released this May as saying Alice Huang was “actually still our boss” and remained involved in daily operations.

The company’s finances have also attracted attention. Singapore corporate records show its total assets rose roughly 90-fold from end-2023 to 2024, reaching almost $3 billion.

In a separate statement Thursday issued under James Tan’s name, Megaspeed said it takes its obligations under U.S. export control laws “extremely seriously.”

The company said it had cooperated with inspections by the U.S. Commerce Department’s Bureau of Industry and Security and was ready to cooperate fully with other relevant U.S. authorities.

2 planes collide on the ground in Miami, only minor injuries reported

📅 Nov 15, 2026  |  Category: Technology  |  Read Source →

The accident involved a Copa Airlines Boeing 737 aircraft with 172 people aboard and a UPS Boeing 767 cargo plane.

MIAMI (AP) â Federal aviation safety officials on Sunday were investigating a minor collision between two planes at a taxiway intersection at Miami International Airport, where one aircraft’s wingtip struck the nose of another, authorities said. Two people reported minor injuries.

The accident happened at about 9 p.m. Saturday and involved a Copa Airlines Boeing 737 aircraft with 172 people aboard and a UPS Boeing 767 cargo plane, according to the National Transportation Safety Board and Copa Airlines. The wing of the Copa flight and the nose of the UPS plane collided, the NTSB said.

Photos taken at the scene show the UPS plane nose with a horizontal gash on one side and a larger hole in the middle.

On audio recorded by ATC.com, a pilot on the UPS plane told air traffic control that he went too far at the intersection and his plane was clipped by the Copa flight. Air traffic control told the UPS pilot that he was supposed to yield to the Copa aircraft.

The NTSB said Sunday that preliminary information showed the UPS plane was instructed to give way to the Copa Airlines flight but did not stop. The UPS aircraft had substantial damage, while an assessment of the Copa plane had not been completed, it said. The NTSB said it opened an investigation and expected a preliminary report to be released within 30 days.

The Copa plane had just arrived from Panama City and the UPS plane was headed to Bogota, Colombia, officials said.

In a statement on social media, Copa called the collision minor and said two of its passengers were treated at the scene for minor injuries.

UPS said it was working with authorities to determine what happened.

“We cannot speculate on the details of the incident,” UPS said in a statement. “The National Transportation Safety Board is responsible for investigating incidents like this and will control the flow of information.”

The mishap came two months after an Amazon cargo plane careened off a runway at Miami International and smashed into a van carrying workers for a company that cleans airplanes, killing five people.

Central African Republic vs Benin - prediction, team news, lineups

📅 Nov 14, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

By Nsidibe Akpan | 14 Nov 2026 23:59

Central African Republic host Benin at the Japoma Stadium in Douala, Cameroon, on Monday in the fourth round of Group F in 2027 Africa Cup of Nations qualifying.

Benin won the first leg 4-1 in Abidjan on Thursday and lead Group F on goal difference, level on seven points with Burkina Faso, while Central African Republic remain bottom with no points and a goal difference of -6.

Central African Republic, known as Les Fauves, have lost all three qualifiers, scoring two goals and conceding eight, and have not won any match since October last year.

Mauritania won 3-1 in their opener on September 24, and Burkina Faso followed with a 1-0 victory over them on September 28.

Head coach Abdelaziz Kerkache, a Moroccan who has previously worked at Mouloudia d’Oujda, was appointed on November 6 to replace Eloge Enza Yamissi, only six days before the first leg.

Kerkache recalled Geoffrey Kondogbia of Olympique de Marseille and Le Mans forward Louis Mafouta, who both missed the opening two qualifiers, and Mafouta responded by equalising in the first leg.

Les Fauves had more of the ball in Abidjan, with 56% possession and eight corners to Benin’s five, yet goalkeeper Dominique Youfeigane faced eight shots on target and conceded four.

Central African Republic are still chasing a first Africa Cup of Nations finals appearance after narrowly missing out in 2021 and 2023, and their home leg is being staged in Douala rather than in Bangui.

Benin, nicknamed Les Guepards, have taken seven points from three Group F games under Gernot Rohr, beginning with a 1-1 draw at Burkina Faso on September 25.

A 2-1 win over Mauritania in Abidjan on September 29 followed, with Rodolfo Aloko scoring either side of half time, as Benin again used an Ivory Coast venue because the Stade de l’Amitie in Cotonou is not yet approved for qualifiers.

Hassane Imourane opened the scoring in the seventh minute of the first leg, Louis Mafouta equalised in the 19th, and Benin were 3-1 up at half time after Aloko struck in the 31st minute and Aiyegun Tosin in the 36th.

Tosin completed his brace in the 56th minute, and he and Aloko have now scored six of Benin’s seven goals in qualifying.

Benin won despite having only 44% of the possession, turning 18 shots into four goals, with eight efforts on target against Central African Republic’s 14 shots and five on target.

Rohr said afterwards that the scoreline flattered his side because Central African Republic attacked a great deal, and he expects a reaction in a rematch only four days later.

Central African Republic Africa Cup of Nations Qualifying form:

For the Central African Republic, Edwin Quarshie, booked in the 1-0 defeat by Burkina Faso, missed the first leg through suspension for accumulated yellow cards and should be available again on Monday.

Venuste Baboula, who has an ankle sprain, was left out of the starting lineup on Thursday, and he could be saved for the return match.

Kondogbia, Mafouta and Goduine Koyalipou all started in Abidjan, and Kerkache has had only one match to work with that group.

For Benin, captain and striker Steve Mounie remains out injured, so Rohr again leans on Aloko, Tosin and Junior Olaitan, who has three goals and seven assists for Besiktas this season.

Benin were far more efficient than their numbers suggest, putting eight of 18 shots on target with only 44% of the ball, and they did it without Mounie.

Central African Republic have conceded eight goals in three qualifiers, and a second game in four days under Kerkache is unlikely to repair a defence that let in three before half time on Thursday.

Benin cannot afford to slip either, because Burkina Faso are also on seven points after an added-time winner from Elohim Kabore against Mauritania, and the Cheetahs lead the table only on goal difference.

A clean sheet is less certain, because Mafouta equalised in the first leg and Kondogbia gives Les Fauves a better platform than they had in September, so we expect Central African Republic to score but not to take anything from the game.

Germany vs Netherlands - prediction, team news, lineups

📅 Nov 14, 2026  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

By Anthony Nolan | 14 Nov 2026 23:59

Fighting for a place in the UEFA Nations League quarter-finals, Germany are set to host Group A2 leaders the Netherlands at Olympiastadion Berlin on Monday.

Die Mannschaft know that they likely need to win to progress, while Oranje through barring an unprecedented swing in goal-difference.

Jurgen Klopp's Germany are pushing to qualify for the Nations League quarter-finals for just the second time, but they face a tough test to reach the last eight.

Die Mannschaft are currently third in Group A2, level on eight points with Greece and three behind the Netherlands, and their chance to go through leans on the fact UEFA prioritises head-to-head points between tied teams.

Klopp's side were beaten 1-0 by the Azure and White on September 27 before drawing 0-0 with them on matchday four.

However, Ivan Jovanovic's men were hammered 7-0 by the Dutch on Friday, meaning that a German victory over the Netherlands on Monday would see them progress ahead of Greece regardless of their result against Serbia.

Die Mannschaft were held to a 1-1 stalemate by Oranje on matchday one, with Cody Gakpo equalising in second-half stoppage time to deny his former Liverpool manager maximum points.

However, Klopp's team have remained undefeated in the group since their loss to Greece, most recently beating Serbia 3-1 on Friday, when they raced into a 2-0 lead within the first 14 minutes thanks to an own-goal from Srdjan Babic and a follow-up by Florian Wirtz.

Meanwhile, Xavi's Netherlands have yet to taste defeat in the Nations League this term, and are in a commanding position at the top of their group.

Top of the pile with 11 points, Oranje's 7-0 demolition of Greece last time out - following a 2-2 draw on October 1 - means the only possibility of them finishing below the top two is if there is a dramatic 14-goal swing in the Azure and White's favour.

Xavi is without talismanic centre-back Virgil van Dijk this month, but his team were still able to keep their first clean sheet in the competition on Friday, while Tijani Reijnders's first-half hat-trick set the tone for his flourishing attack.

However, it remains to be seen whether the Netherlands can repeat that feat in Germany, considering that they have won just one of their last six clashes with Die Mannschaft as the away side.

On the other hand, Oranje are unbeaten in their last seven matches on the road, last suffered a penalty shootout defeat against Spain in March 2025.

Germany have no new injury issues to contend with, though they are missing Bayern Munich stars Jamal Musiala and Serge Gnabry due to respective muscle problems.

In their absence, Klopp could continue with Brajan Gruda, Florian Wirtz and Derry Scherhant behind Arsenal man Kai Havertz up top.

As for the Netherlands, they were forced to take off Frenkie De Jong at half time against Greece after he suffered back pain, and he has now left the national squad to return to Barcelona.

Teun Koopmeiners looks set to step in and fill the gap alongside Joey Veerman at the base of Xavi's midfield, while Tijani Reijnders operates in the number 10 role.

Further forward, Donyell Malen has been a standout for Roma this season with 12 goals across all competitions, and he should lead the line on Monday while Cody Gakpo and Crysencio Summerville provide width.

Captain Virgil van Dijk has sat out this international break with complaints of physical discomfort, so expect to see Tottenham Hotspur's Micky van de Ven and Jan Paul van Hecker start at centre-back.

Ter Stegen; Treu, Thiaw, Tah, Mittelstadt; Kimmich, Pavlovic; Gruda, Wirtz, Scherhant; Havertz

Verbruggen; Dumfries, Van Hecke, Van de Ven, Timber; Veerman, Koopmeiners; Summerville, Reijnders, Gakpo; Malen

The maths of the table is complex, but Germany have a simple task on Monday - they will likely need to beat the Netherlands to progress.

Oranje have yet to lose in the Nations League this season, though with the stakes lowered, they could be caught off guard.

Contenders Hall Of Fame Honoree Peter Farrelly Talks ‘I Play Rocky’, ‘Dumb & Dumber’, The Hair Gel Bit In ‘There’s Something About Mary’ & More

📅 Nov 14, 2026  |  Category: Entertainment  |  Read Source →

Prolific director, screenwriter and producer Peter Farrelly reflected on some of his most famous films Saturday at Deadline’s Contenders Film Los Angeles event.

Contenders Hall of Fame honoree Farrelly talked with Deadline’s Mike Fleming Jr about his most recent film I Play Rocky and the process of bringing it to the screen. Earlier, Farrelly had appeared on the film’s panel with star Anthony Ippolito, who plays Sylvester Stallone in the film, and talked about the uphill battle to making the crowd-pleasing underdog tale.

Farrelly revealed that it was his daughter’s ex-boyfriend who first read the script and told Farrelly it “was the best script he had ever read.” Farrelly agreed and pushed forward with the film, noting it was imperative that he had Stallone’s approval on the script, which came quickly.

He also shared his own “Rocky” moment: “The bravest thing I ever did in my life was decide to write when I was in my mid-20s,” he said.

RELATED: ‘I Play Rocky’s Peter Farrelly Talks Why The Making Of ‘Rocky’ Was The Next Real-Life Story He Wanted To Take A Swing At – Toronto Studio

Farrelly also talked about his years and years of getting turned down for scripts and having to push through the rejection and not give up. As for 1994’s Dumb & Dumber, “We wrote that script five years before it got made, and it was the exact same script,” he shared. “Every studio passed, every actor passed.” He acknowledged that he changed the title to A Power Tool Is Not a Toy and “everyone started reading it.” It was the eventual film’s star Jim Carrey who told him Dumb & Dumber is the title, “so we went back to that,” he said.

“You’ve got to believe,, that they’re wrong and you’re right,” he said regarding the rejections. “You know, year after year getting turned down, it’s hard. You just gotta stick out and hope that you’re right.”

RELATED: ‘I Play Rocky’ Review: Peter Farrelly’s Sensational Biopic Of The Making Of Stallone’s Classic Is Just As Inspiring As The Original Movie – Toronto Film Festival

One of the most memorable and hilarious scenes in Farrelly’s films was in There’s Something About Mary and Cameron Diaz’s “hair gel.” “I realized we were going to have to go to R [in that film] because we were right on the PG-13 line.

“There’s two things that the studio didn’t want was the zipper joke in the beginning and the hair gel thing of course, and we understood that, especially the hair gel.” But of course, the hair gel stayed in and the rest is history.

"Each one of these cards is this window into this incredible world. It's not just world building—it's worlds building."

When you purchase through links on our site, we may earn an affiliate commission. Here’s how it works.

Netflix's Magic: The Gathering series was initially announced in 2019 with the MCU's Russo Brothers at the helm, though they left a couple of years later thanks to the all-purpose "creative differences". That was all we heard until 2024 until Brandon Routh, who had been cast as planeswalker Gideon Jura, casually mentioned in an unrelated interview that the project had been binned.

Netflix announced soon after that actually the animated series was a go, now with Terry Matalas of Star Trek: Picard and the 12 Monkeys TV series as its head showrunner, with a teaser image showing pyromancer Chandra and leonin Ajani Goldmane silhouetted against a red sky.

Now, a mere seven years after the first reveal, there's a "sneak peek" trailer with a bunch of excited Wizards of the Coast staff, art labeled "early development", and a few seconds of Ajani and Chandra doing their stuff.

The most intriguing part is when, over a montage of 3D props including a car that looks like an art deco Batmobile from New Capenna, executive producer and supervising director Patrick Osborne says, "All the planes exist in their own pocket universe. That comes with culture, design language, architecture, and we're even making the film-making style different depending on where it is."

That's a good idea. An animated series with different art styles for each plane the characters travel to would be a great way of representing Magic, a card game where every artist gets to put their own spin on how things look. "Each one of these cards is this window into this incredible world," Matalas says in the trailer. "It's not just world building—it's worlds building."

As for the plot, Netflix's release sums it up as Chandra and a party of planeswalkers trying to prevent a war from consuming the entire multiverse. So a nicely low-stakes story then. It's scheduled for release in 2027. There's a live-action Magic: The Gathering movie still apparently in the works as well.

Declan Rice delivers verdict on £100m Arsenal transfer target after ‘contact made’

📅 Nov 14, 2026  |  Category: World  |  Read Source →

Your weekly briefing on Arsenal, featuring team news and expert analysis from Matt Verri

I would like to be emailed about offers, event and updates from Evening Standard. Read our privacy notice.

Declan Rice insists that he is relishing the competition from Alex Scott with England, as Arsenal are linked with a potential big-money transfer swoop for the Bournemouth star.

Scott continues to go from strength to strength for both club and country, earning his first senior caps for the Three Lions during the previous international break in recognition for his fine form in the Premier League.

The 23-year-old made his England debut off the bench in September’s 2-0 away win over the Czech Republic in the Nations League, with Rice having pulled out of the squad, before then being handed his maiden start by Thomas Tuchel in the 7-0 demolition of Croatia last month.

Scott picked up a thigh injury during that camp that it was feared could keep him out for up to two months, though the issue was not as serious as first feared and he was back on the Bournemouth bench in their last game against Ipswich.

While Scott has been unable to press his claims for further England involvement during the current international window, Rice knows that he will be quickly back challenging for a regular starting role in Tuchel’s engine room.

“To be honest, Alex Scott playing was incredible,” Rice said of Scott ahead of Sunday night’s crunch clash with world champions Spain in Madrid, per The Mirror. “The way he's been playing for Bournemouth the last two or three seasons has been remarkable.

“The way he's being spoken about is totally right because of the levels he's set for himself and I totally understand playing for England comes with competition.

“When we [Arsenal] signed Bruno Guimaraes, when we signed [Martin] Zubimendi, when we had Myles [Lewis-Skelly], [Mikel] Merino, [Martin] Odegaard, you're competing with six midfielders at your club every day so for me there's nothing different.

“Throughout my whole career there's been competition. It's normal.”

Rice could yet line up with Scott at club level, with reports last week claiming that Arsenal have joined Chelsea and Liverpool in the race for his services ahead of the January transfer window.

The i Paper claimed that all three clubs had made initial contact with Bournemouth over possible moves for the former Bristol City star, with Chelsea seeing a number of summer bids rejected for a player who has turned down multiple offers from his current employers to extend a contract that is due to expire in June 2028.

Young talent will be trusted, Bruno Fernandes can thrive and touchline presence is vital - what Man United fans can learn as incoming boss Mauricio Pochettino masterminds 6-0 win for USA

📅 2026-11-14  |  Category: Sports  |  Read Source →

Published: 18:47 EST, 14 November 2026 | Updated: 19:37 EST, 14 November 2026

Mauricio Pochettino enjoyed a stellar penultimate game in charge of the United States, thrashing Haiti 6-0.

The newly appointed Manchester United manager has just one game left in charge of the USA before getting to work at Old Trafford.

He will link up with United on Wednesday, but first must navigate the second leg of their Concacaf Nations League quarter-final tie with Haiti on Tuesday.

Here, Pochettino cut a fairly reserved figure on the touchline as he was rewarded for trusting youth - and even saw his team score from a corner, something United don't do much of these days.

Without further ado, here are four things Manchester United fans learned from the win.

Mauricio Pochettino, dressed in a scarf, was fairly reserved as the United States beat Haiti 6-0

Pochettino’s arrival spells good news for United’s proud tradition of promoting homegrown youth - it is glad tidings for the likes of Shea Lacey and Kobbie Mainoo. You wonder if, for a while, JJ Gabriel will look on forlornly from Real Madrid’s reserves.

The Argentinian handed out 13 debuts in the September-October international break, including to eight teenagers in one 4-1 victory over Peru.

His strategy was no different here as he started 18-year-old Julian Hall up front and Diego Kochen, 20, in goal - both players he has given debuts to this year. He was rewarded, with Hall scoring his fourth goal in just five games for the United States, while Kochen kept a clean sheet.

Despite his affinity for the stars of tomorrow, he is a hard schoolmaster. In a column for The Athletic in 2022, he revealed that he sat a young Harry Kane down for a brutal meeting where he showed him stats and clips to justify why he wasn’t playing him more.

Pochettino had known Kane’s potential from their first training session but wanted the ‘tough conversation’ to get the best out of him. Kane went on to score 169 goals under him for Tottenham, and now he’s the best player in the world.

His trust in fresh faces could inject some much-needed enthusiasm around the club, strengthen the squad's connection with the fanbase, and increase the energy levels in one of the Premier League's oldest, most lethargic teams.

The likes of Shea Lacey will be delighted at how Pochettino puts trust in younger players

Julian Hall, 18, struck his fourth goal in five games for the United States under Pochettino

Pochettino has a history of getting the most out of attacking midfielders such as Dele Alli, Christian Eriksen, and Adam Lallana. You can add Cole Palmer to that list too, though he was primarily a right winger under him at Chelsea.

Manchester United desperately need him to get a tune out of Bruno Fernandes, who has cut a frustrated figure this season and whose creative efficiency has declined - though he still has nine goal involvements.

If Christian Pulisic’s performance against Haiti is anything to go by, Fernandes will be happy. The attacking midfielder scored twice, playing a free role and being given penalty duty.

The 28-year-old was also asked to muck in defensively, and that’s something Fernandes is willing to do. Across the seasons, he has often covered more ground than any of his team-mates.

Pochettino and Pulisic have seemingly had a distant relationship, despite the latter’s insistence to the contrary. The manager branded it ‘b*******’ when the AC Milan star opted out of the Gold Cup last year, and left him out of his selection a couple of months ago, bringing in 16-year-old Cavan Sullivan.

Asked about Pochettino's upcoming final game, Pulisic said at full-time: 'We're treating it like any other game.' No emotion on display.

Depending on how you interpret Fernandes - either a die-for-the-badge club servant or a primadonna - Pochettino could draw the best or worst out of the Portuguese.

Pochettino often gets the best out of attacking midfielders - will he work well with Bruno Fernandes?

He has had a distant relationship with Christian Pulisic, but the AC Milan star scored twice here

Pochettino tends to push his defensive lines high up the pitch and his strategy with the United States has been no different.

They were almost caught out for trying to play the offside trap early on as a simple ball from midfield sent Danley Jean Jacques through one-on-one, forcing Kochen into a good save.

Meanwhile, full-backs Antonee Robinson and Alex Freeman were constantly pushing high and overlapping in his 4-2-3-1 system - although you’d obviously expect a strong level of intent when they’re favourites against Haiti.

Given his high-line tradition, Pochettino may well favour more mobile defenders such as Leny Yoro and Patrick Dorgu.

The battle for Harry Maguire will be to convince his new boss that his other qualities compensate for his lack of pace.

Pochettino takes inspiration from the frenetic football of Marcelo Bielsa, who managed him at Newell’s Old Boys in Argentina.

But he'll have to balance his philosophy with the reality of United's woeful defensive record this campaign. Just the 15 goals conceded in the last five games...

Pochettino showed an eagerness to intervene early in the game - a possible boost after Michael Carrick suffered from slow starts

Pochettino is typically seen as an animated character on the touchline, in contrast to Carrick's statesmanlike behaviour, but he spent lots of time in his seat here.

He has calmed down somewhat these days - though he did kick a crate of bottles in frustration at the World Cup - and didn’t even react much when his team scored.

A major criticism of Carrick is that he takes too long to change things in-game, often going late with substitutions. Pochettino has traditionally leaned that way, too, but he was eager to influence proceedings here.

Even three minutes in, at a break while Antonee Robinson was being treated, he called Pulisic over for what looked like an intense tactical chat.

United have been plagued by slow starts this season, so it was encouraging to see Pochettino so alert and full of ideas early into the game. He could have coasted, really, but wanted to eke out every advantage.
